68: function beginCleanShutdown(reason="user-close") {
69:   if (shutdownStarted) return;
70:   shutdownStarted = true;
71:   try { appendShutdownLog(`shutdown-start:${reason}:pid=${process.pid}:windows=${BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length}`); } catch {}
72:   destroyAuxiliaryWindows(reason);
73:   // Give Electron a short grace period to release renderer/GPU resources. If the
74:   // normal quit sequence stalls, force only THIS Electron process to exit.
75:   setTimeout(() => {
76:     try { appendShutdownLog(`shutdown-force-exit:${reason}:windows=${BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length}`); } catch {}
77:     try { app.exit(0); } catch {}
78:   }, 1500).unref?.();

183: function parseTimeRange(text) {
184:   const m=String(text||"").match(/(?:^|\s)([01]?\d|2[0-3])[:：](\d{2})\s*(?:[-~～]|부터)\s*([01]?\d|2[0-3])[:：](\d{2})/);
185:   if(m) return [`${m[1].padStart(2,"0")}:${m[2]}`,`${m[3].padStart(2,"0")}:${m[4]}`];
186:   const one=String(text||"").match(/(?:^|\s)([01]?\d|2[0-3])[:：](\d{2})/);
187:   return one ? [`${one[1].padStart(2,"0")}:${one[2]}`,""] : ["",""];
188: }
189: function classifyCalendarStatus(text) {
190:   const value=String(text||"");
191:   if(/취소|폐지/.test(value)) return "취소";
192:   if(/확정/.test(value)) return "확정";
193:   if(/예약|대관|사용/.test(value)) return "예약";
194:   if(/예정|계획|공고|제출기간/.test(value)) return "예정";
195:   return "상태 미기재";
196: }
197: function parseOwner(text) {
198:   const m=String(text||"").match(/^\(([^)]+)\)/);
199:   const token=(m?.[1]||"").trim();
200:   if(!token) return ["",""];
201:   if(/부$|부\s/.test(token)) return [token.replace(/\s*확정$/,""),""];
202:   const parts=token.split(/\s+/);
203:   if(parts.length>1 && /부$/.test(parts[0])) return [parts[0],parts.slice(1).join(" ")];
204:   return ["",token.replace(/\s*확정$/," ").trim()];
205: }
206: function parseSchoolCalendarMatrix(matrix, year, month, sheetName) {
207:   const rows=Array.isArray(matrix)?matrix:[]; const result=[];
208:   for(let r=0;r<rows.length;r++){
209:     const row=rows[r]||[];
210:     for(let c=0;c<row.length;c++){
211:       const cell=String(row[c]??"").trim();
212:       const dm=cell.match(/(?:^|\s)(\d{1,2})[.월\/-](\d{1,2})[.일\/-]?\s*\([일월화수목금토]\)/);
213:       if(!dm) continue;
214:       const date=isoDate(Number(year),Number(dm[1]),Number(dm[2]));
215:       let content="";
216:       for(let rr=r+1;rr<=Math.min(r+3,rows.length-1);rr++){
217:         const candidate=String((rows[rr]||[])[c]??"").trim();
218:         if(candidate && candidate!=="내용" && !candidate.startsWith("* 작성양식")){content=candidate;break;}
219:       }
220:       if(!content) continue;
221:       for(const block of splitCalendarBlocks(content)){
222:         if(!block || /^내용$/.test(block)) continue;
223:         const [start,end]=parseTimeRange(block); const [dept,manager]=parseOwner(block);
224:         const lines=block.split(/\n|\//).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
225:         const cleaned=lines.map(x=>x.replace(/^\([^)]+\)\s*/,"").replace(/(?:[01]?\d|2[0-3])[:：]\d{2}(?:\s*[-~～]\s*(?:[01]?\d|2[0-3])[:：]\d{2})?/g,"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
226:         let title=cleaned.find(x=>!/^(1|2|3)학년|희망|전체|각 반|세미나실|고당예관|교실|운영위원실|가온누리실/.test(x)) || cleaned[0] || block.slice(0,60);
227:         title=title.replace(/^[-/\s]+|[-/\s]+$/g,"").slice(0,120);
228:         const target=lines.find(x=>/(학년|학생|교사|학부모|희망자|전교생|전체)/.test(x) && !x.includes(title)) || "";
229:         const place=lines.find(x=>/(실|관|교실|센터|캠퍼스|학교|체육관|세미나|다목적|운영위원)/.test(x) && !x.includes(title) && x!==target) || "";
230:         const originalId=crypto.createHash("sha1").update(`${sheetName}|${date}|${block}`).digest("hex").slice(0,18);
231:         result.push([`SCH-${originalId}`,year,date,start,end,title,target,place,dept,manager,"학교 공통",originalId,classifyCalendarStatus(block),new Date().toISOString(),"자동동기화",block,block,"FALSE","","",""]);
232:       }
233:     }
234:   }
235:   const seen=new Set(); return result.filter(row=>{const k=row[0];if(seen.has(k))return false;seen.add(k);return true;});
236: }

263: function validateServiceAccount(value) {
264:   return Boolean(
265:     value && value.type === "service_account" &&
266:     typeof value.client_email === "string" &&
267:     typeof value.private_key === "string" &&
268:     value.private_key.startsWith("-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----") &&
269:     value.token_uri === "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token"
270:   );

273: function recoveryKey(salt) {
274:   // safeStorage가 Windows/Chromium 키 문제로 복호화에 실패할 때만 사용하는 로컬 복구키입니다.
275:   // 동일 Windows 사용자/PC에서 재부팅 후에도 안정적으로 재생성됩니다.
276:   const identity = `${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-CREDENTIAL-RECOVERY-v1`;
277:   return crypto.scryptSync(identity, salt, 32);
278: }
279: 
280: async function writeCredentialRecovery(value) {
281:   const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16), iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);
282:   const key = recoveryKey(salt);
283:   const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", key, iv);
284:   const encrypted = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value), "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
285:   const payload = {
286:     version: 1, algorithm: "aes-256-gcm",
287:     salt: salt.toString("base64"), iv: iv.toString("base64"),
288:     tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString("base64"), data: encrypted.toString("base64")
289:   };
290:   await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(credentialRecoveryPath()), { recursive: true });
291:   await fs.writeFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), JSON.stringify(payload), "utf8");
292: }
293: 
294: async function readCredentialRecovery() {
295:   const payload = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), "utf8"));
296:   if (!payload || payload.version !== 1 || payload.algorithm !== "aes-256-gcm") throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
297:   const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"), iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"), tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"), data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
298:   const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", recoveryKey(salt), iv);
299:   decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
300:   return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data), decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
301: }

303: async function saveEncrypted(filePath, value) {
304:   await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
305:   if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
306:     await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(value)));
307:   } else {
308:     // 인증정보는 아래 복구 저장소가 있으므로, 다른 보안값은 기존처럼 실패시킵니다.
309:     if (path.resolve(filePath) !== path.resolve(credentialPath())) throw new Error("Windows 보안 저장소를 사용할 수 없습니다.");
310:   }
311:   if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) await writeCredentialRecovery(value);
312: }

314: // __UEP_GOOGLE_MISSING_CREDENTIAL_08069__
315: async function readEncrypted(filePath) {
316:   let encrypted = null;
317:   try { encrypted = await fs.readFile(filePath); } catch (readError) {
318:     if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) {
319:       try { return await readCredentialRecovery(); } catch {}
320:     }
321:     if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath()) && (readError?.code === "ENOENT" || /no such file/i.test(String(readError?.message||"")))) {
322:       const friendly = new Error("담임 배포 PC의 Google 시트 연결 승인이 아직 준비되지 않았습니다.");
323:       friendly.code = "UEP_GOOGLE_CREDENTIAL_MISSING";
324:       friendly.setupRequired = true;
325:       throw friendly;
326:     }
327:     throw readError;
328:   }
329:   if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
330:     try { return JSON.parse(safeStorage.decryptString(encrypted)); } catch {}
331:   }
332:   // 일부 구버전/수동 이관 파일이 평문 JSON인 경우 현재 형식으로 재저장합니다.
333:   try {
334:     const legacy = JSON.parse(encrypted.toString("utf8"));
335:     if (legacy && typeof legacy === "object") { await saveEncrypted(filePath, legacy); return legacy; }
336:   } catch {}
337:   // Google 서비스 계정은 safeStorage가 깨져도 동일 PC/사용자용 AES-GCM 복구본으로 자동 복원합니다.
338:   if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) {
339:     try {
340:       const recovered = await readCredentialRecovery();
341:       if (validateServiceAccount(recovered)) {
342:         try { if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(recovered))); } catch {}
343:         return recovered;
344:       }
345:     } catch {}
346:   }
347:   const backup = `${filePath}.unreadable-${Date.now()}.bak`;
348:   try { await fs.rename(filePath, backup); } catch {}
349:   const error = new Error("저장된 보안 인증정보를 복호화할 수 없습니다. 서비스 계정 JSON을 다시 등록해 주세요.");
350:   error.code = "UEP_SAFE_STORAGE_DECRYPT_FAILED";
351:   throw error;
352: }

354: function base64url(value) { return Buffer.from(value).toString("base64url"); }

357: const UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hkhsm00-k81c/UEP-Updates/main/uep-policy.json";
358: let googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:"",expiresAt:0};

360: function googleOAuthError(message,code){const e=new Error(message);e.code=code;return e;}
361: async function loadGoogleOAuthClientId(){
362:   if(googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId && googleOAuthPolicyCache.expiresAt>Date.now()) return googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId;
363:   let response;
364:   try{ response=await net.fetch(UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL,{cache:"no-store"}); }
365:   catch(error){ throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결 설정을 확인하지 못했습니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE"); }
366:   if(!response.ok) throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 연결 설정 조회 실패 (${response.status})`,"UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE");
367:   const policy=await response.json();
368:   const clientId=String(policy?.googleOAuthClientId||"").trim();
369:   if(!/\.apps\.googleusercontent\.com$/i.test(clientId)) throw googleOAuthError("관리자가 UEP Google OAuth 클라이언트를 아직 설정하지 않았습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_NOT_CONFIGURED");
370:   googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId,expiresAt:Date.now()+10*60*1000};
371:   return clientId;
372: }
373: function googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt){
374:   const identity=`${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-GOOGLE-USER-OAUTH-v1`;
375:   return crypto.scryptSync(identity,salt,32);
376: }
377: async function writeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(value){
378:   const salt=crypto.randomBytes(16),iv=crypto.randomBytes(12),key=googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt);
379:   const cipher=crypto.createCipheriv('aes-256-gcm',key,iv);
380:   const encrypted=Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value),'utf8'),cipher.final()]);
381:   const payload={version:1,algorithm:'aes-256-gcm',salt:salt.toString('base64'),iv:iv.toString('base64'),tag:cipher.getAuthTag().toString('base64'),data:encrypted.toString('base64')};
382:   await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()),{recursive:true});
383:   await fs.writeFile(googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath(),JSON.stringify(payload),'utf8');
384: }
385: async function readGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(){
386:   const payload=JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath(),'utf8'));
387:   if(!payload||payload.version!==1||payload.algorithm!=='aes-256-gcm')throw new Error('invalid OAuth recovery token');
388:   const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,'base64'),iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,'base64'),tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,'base64'),data=Buffer.from(payload.data,'base64');
389:   const decipher=crypto.createDecipheriv('aes-256-gcm',googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt),iv);decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
390:   return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data),decipher.final()]).toString('utf8'));
391: }
392: async function saveGoogleUserOAuth(value){
393:   await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(googleUserOAuthPath()),{recursive:true});
394:   let primarySaved=false;
395:   if(safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()){
396:     try{await fs.writeFile(googleUserOAuthPath(),safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(value)));primarySaved=true;}catch{}
397:   }
398:   await writeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(value);
399:   return {primarySaved,recoverySaved:true};
400: }
401: async function readGoogleUserOAuth(){
402:   let primaryExists=false;
403:   try{
404:     const encrypted=await fs.readFile(googleUserOAuthPath());primaryExists=true;
405:     if(safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()){
406:       try{return JSON.parse(safeStorage.decryptString(encrypted));}catch{}
407:     }
408:     try{const legacy=JSON.parse(encrypted.toString('utf8'));if(legacy&&typeof legacy==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(legacy);return legacy;}}catch{}
409:   }catch(error){if(error?.code!=='ENOENT'&&!/no such file/i.test(String(error?.message||''))){} }
410:   try{
411:     const recovered=await readGoogleUserOAuthRecovery();
412:     if(recovered&&typeof recovered==='object'){
413:       if(safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable())try{await fs.writeFile(googleUserOAuthPath(),safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(recovered)));}catch{}
414:       return recovered;
415:     }
416:   }catch{}
417:   if(primaryExists){try{await fs.rename(googleUserOAuthPath(),`${googleUserOAuthPath()}.unreadable-${Date.now()}.bak`);}catch{}}
418:   return null;
419: }
420: async function googleUserInfo(accessToken){
421:   const response=await net.fetch('https://openidconnect.googleapis.com/v1/userinfo',{headers:{authorization:`Bearer ${accessToken}`}});
422:   if(!response.ok) throw googleOAuthError(`Google 사용자 확인 실패 (${response.status})`,'UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_USERINFO_FAILED');
423:   return await response.json();
424: }
425: async function refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved){
426:   if(!saved?.refresh_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
427:   const clientId=String(saved.client_id||'').trim() || await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();
428:   const body=new URLSearchParams({client_id:clientId,refresh_token:String(saved.refresh_token),grant_type:'refresh_token'});
429:   const response=await net.fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},body});
430:   if(!response.ok){let detail='';try{detail=(await response.json())?.error_description||'';}catch{};throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 토큰 갱신 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:''}`,"UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");}
431:   const next=await response.json();
432:   const merged={...saved,...next,client_id:clientId,expires_at:Date.now()+Math.max(60,Number(next.expires_in||3600))*1000,updated_at:new Date().toISOString()};
433:   await saveGoogleUserOAuth(merged);
434:   return merged;
435: }
436: async function getGoogleUserSheetsToken(){
437:   let saved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
438:   if(!saved) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결이 필요합니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
439:   if(saved.access_token && Number(saved.expires_at||0)>Date.now()+90000) return String(saved.access_token);
440:   saved=await refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved);
441:   if(!saved.access_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 액세스 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
442:   return String(saved.access_token);
443: }
444: async function getReadonlySheetsAuth(credentials=null){

457: async function authorizeGoogleUser({loginHint=''}={}){
458:   const clientId=await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();
459:   const verifier=crypto.randomBytes(48).toString('base64url');
460:   const challenge=crypto.createHash('sha256').update(verifier).digest('base64url');
461:   const state=crypto.randomBytes(24).toString('base64url');
462:   let timer=null;
463:   let settled=false;
464:   let callbackResolve,callbackReject;
465:   const callbackPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{callbackResolve=resolve;callbackReject=reject;});
466:   const server=http.createServer((req,res)=>{
467:     try{
468:       const u=new URL(req.url||'/', 'http://127.0.0.1');
469:       if(u.pathname!=='/oauth2callback'){res.writeHead(404);res.end('Not found');return;}
470:       const returnedState=String(u.searchParams.get('state')||'');
471:       const code=String(u.searchParams.get('code')||'');
472:       const error=String(u.searchParams.get('error')||'');
473:       res.writeHead(200,{'content-type':'text/html; charset=utf-8','cache-control':'no-store'});
474:       if(error||returnedState!==state||!code){
475:         res.end('<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><title>UEP Google 연결</title><h2>UEP Google 계정 연결이 취소되었거나 실패했습니다.</h2><p>이 창을 닫고 UEP에서 다시 시도해 주세요.</p>');
476:         if(!settled){settled=true;callbackReject(googleOAuthError(error?`Google 승인 취소: ${error}`:'Google 승인 응답 검증에 실패했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CALLBACK_FAILED'));}
477:         return;
478:       }
479:       res.end('<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><title>UEP Google 연결</title><h2>Google 승인 정보가 UEP로 전달되었습니다.</h2><p>이 창을 닫고 UEP로 돌아가 연결 완료 여부를 확인해 주세요.</p>');
480:       if(!settled){settled=true;callbackResolve(code);}
481:     }catch(error){if(!settled){settled=true;callbackReject(error);}}
482:   });
483:   try{
484:     await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{server.once('error',reject);server.listen(0,'127.0.0.1',resolve);});
485:     const address=server.address();
486:     const port=typeof address==='object'&&address?address.port:0;
487:     if(!port) throw googleOAuthError('Google 승인용 로컬 포트를 열지 못했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CALLBACK_FAILED');
488:     const redirectUri=`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/oauth2callback`;
489:     const authUrl=new URL('https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth');
490:     authUrl.searchParams.set('client_id',clientId);
491:     authUrl.searchParams.set('redirect_uri',redirectUri);
492:     authUrl.searchParams.set('response_type','code');
493:     authUrl.searchParams.set('scope','openid email https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets.readonly');
494:     authUrl.searchParams.set('code_challenge',challenge);
495:     authUrl.searchParams.set('code_challenge_method','S256');
496:     authUrl.searchParams.set('state',state);
497:     authUrl.searchParams.set('access_type','offline');
498:     authUrl.searchParams.set('prompt','consent');
499:     if(String(loginHint||'').includes('@')) authUrl.searchParams.set('login_hint',String(loginHint).trim());
500:     await shell.openExternal(authUrl.toString());
501:     timer=setTimeout(()=>{if(!settled){settled=true;callbackReject(googleOAuthError('Google 계정 승인이 시간 초과되었습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_TIMEOUT'));}},180000);
502:     const code=await callbackPromise;
503:     const body=new URLSearchParams({client_id:clientId,code,code_verifier:verifier,redirect_uri:redirectUri,grant_type:'authorization_code'});
504:     const response=await net.fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},body});
505:     if(!response.ok){let detail='';try{detail=(await response.json())?.error_description||'';}catch{};throw googleOAuthError(`Google 토큰 교환 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:''}`,'UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_TOKEN_FAILED');}
506:     const tokenPayload=await response.json();
507:     if(!tokenPayload.access_token) throw googleOAuthError('Google 액세스 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_TOKEN_FAILED');
508:     const info=await googleUserInfo(tokenPayload.access_token);
509:     const saved={...tokenPayload,client_id:clientId,email:String(info?.email||''),sub:String(info?.sub||''),expires_at:Date.now()+Math.max(60,Number(tokenPayload.expires_in||3600))*1000,created_at:new Date().toISOString(),updated_at:new Date().toISOString()};
510:     await saveGoogleUserOAuth(saved);
511:     liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;
512:     let syncOk=false, syncError='', syncCode='', data=null;
513:     try {
514:       data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
515:       syncOk=true;
516:     } catch(error) {
517:       syncError=String(error?.message||error||'Google 시트 조회 실패');
518:       syncCode=String(error?.code||'');
519:     }
520:     return {ok:true,authorized:true,mode:'user_oauth',account:saved.email,sourceName:data?.sourceName||UEP_SPREADSHEET_NAME,preview:data?.summary||null,syncedAt:data?.syncedAt||'',syncOk,syncError,syncCode};
521:   }finally{
522:     if(timer)clearTimeout(timer);
523:     try{server.close();}catch{}
524:   }
525: }
526: async function disconnectGoogleUser(){
527:   for(const file of [googleUserOAuthPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){
528:     try{await fs.unlink(file);}catch(error){if(error?.code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
529:   }
530:   liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true};
531: }
532: async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
533:   const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
534:   const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
535:   const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
536:     iss: credentials.client_email,
537:     scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
538:     aud: credentials.token_uri,
539:     iat: now,
540:     exp: now + 3600,
541:   }));
542:   const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
543:   const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
544:   const body = new URLSearchParams({
545:     grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
546:     assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
547:   });
548:   const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
549:     method: "POST",
550:     headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
551:     body,
552:   });
553:   if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
554:   const payload = await response.json();
555:   if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
556:   return payload.access_token;
557: }

559: async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
560:   const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
561:   url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
562:   url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
563:   url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
564:   ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
565:   const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
566:   if (!response.ok) {
567:     let detail = "";
568:     try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
569:     if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
570:       const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
571:       const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
572:       await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
573:       return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
574:     }
575:     throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
576:   }
577:   return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
578: }

580: function sheetNameFromRange(range) {
581:   const text = String(range || "");
582:   const beforeBang = text.split("!")[0];
583:   return beforeBang.replace(/^'/, "").replace(/'$/, "").replace(/''/g, "'");
584: }

586: async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
587:   const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
588:   url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
589:   const response = await fetch(url, {
590:     method: "PUT",
591:     headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
592:     body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
593:   });
594:   if (!response.ok) {
595:     let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
596:     if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
597:       const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
598:       const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
599:       await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
600:       return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
601:     }
602:     throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
603:   }
604:   return response.json();
605: }

607: async function batchUpdateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, data, attempt=0) {
608:   const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
609:   if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
610:   const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchUpdate`);
611:   const response=await fetch(url,{
612:     method:"POST",
613:     headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"Content-Type":"application/json"},
614:     body:JSON.stringify({valueInputOption:"USER_ENTERED",data:items.map(x=>({range:x.range,majorDimension:"ROWS",values:x.values}))}),
615:   });
616:   if(!response.ok){
617:     let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||"";}catch{}
618:     if((response.status===429||response.status>=500)&&attempt<4){
619:       const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
620:       const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
621:       await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
622:       return batchUpdateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,items,attempt+1);
623:     }
624:     throw new Error(`구글시트 일괄 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
625:   }
626:   return response.json();
627: }

630: function columnLetter(index) {
631:   let n=Number(index)+1, out="";
632:   while(n>0){ const r=(n-1)%26; out=String.fromCharCode(65+r)+out; n=Math.floor((n-1)/26); }
633:   return out;
634: }
635: function normalizeSheetHeader(value){return String(value??"").replace(/\r?\n/g," ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();}
636: function findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,required){
637:   const wanted=required.map(x=>String(x));
638:   const limit=Math.min(Array.isArray(matrix)?matrix.length:0,12);
639:   for(let i=0;i<limit;i+=1){
640:     const row=(matrix[i]||[]).map(normalizeSheetHeader);
641:     if(wanted.every(name=>row.includes(name))) return {index:i,headers:row};
642:   }
643:   return null;
644: }
645: async function saveStudentContact(payload={}) {
646:   const allowed={phone:["학생연락처"],email:["학생이메일","이메일","이메일주소"],guardian1Phone:["보호자1연락처"],guardian2Phone:["보호자2연락처"]};
647:   const field=String(payload.field||"");
648:   if(!allowed[field]) throw new Error("수정할 수 없는 학생정보 항목입니다.");
649:   const role=String(payload.role||"");
650:   if(!["admin","homeroom"].includes(role)) throw new Error("학생 연락정보 수정 권한이 없습니다.");
651:   const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
652:   if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
653:   const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
654:   const range="'04_학생연락식별정보'!A1:Z1000";
655:   const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
656:   const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
657:   const header=findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,["학생ID"]);
658:   if(!header) throw new Error("04_학생연락식별정보의 헤더에서 학생ID를 찾지 못했습니다.");
659:   const studentId=String(payload.studentId||"").trim();
660:   if(!studentId) throw new Error("학생ID가 없어 저장할 수 없습니다.");
661:   const idCol=header.headers.indexOf("학생ID");
662:   let fieldCol=-1;
663:   for(const name of allowed[field]){ const idx=header.headers.indexOf(name); if(idx>=0){fieldCol=idx;break;} }
664:   if(fieldCol<0) throw new Error(`04_학생연락식별정보에 ${allowed[field][0]} 열이 없습니다.`);
665:   let dataRow=-1;
666:   for(let r=header.index+1;r<matrix.length;r+=1){ if(String(matrix[r]?.[idCol]||"").trim()===studentId){dataRow=r;break;} }
667:   const value=String(payload.value??"").trim();
668:   if(dataRow<0){
669:     const row=Array(Math.max(header.headers.length,fieldCol+1)).fill("");
670:     row[idCol]=studentId; row[fieldCol]=value;
671:     const useCol=header.headers.indexOf("현재사용여부"); if(useCol>=0)row[useCol]="Y";
672:     await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'04_학생연락식별정보'!A:Z",[row]);
673:   }else{
674:     const sheetRow=dataRow+1;
675:     const col=columnLetter(fieldCol);
676:     await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'04_학생연락식별정보'!${col}${sheetRow}:${col}${sheetRow}`,[[value]]);
677:   }
678:   liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
679:   const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
680:   return {ok:true,data};
681: }

684: async function saveProgramOperation(payload={}) {

803: async function clearSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range) {
804:   const url = `https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:clear`;
805:   const response = await fetch(url, {
806:     method: "POST",
807:     headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
808:     body: "{}",
809:   });
810:   if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공결 삭제 실패 (${response.status})`);
811:   return response.json();
812: }

834: async function saveOfficialAttendance(payload={}) {
835:   const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
836:   if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");

844:   if(id){
845:     const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===id);
846:     if(pos>=0) sheetRow=pos+4;

1150: async function openInputCenterFolder(){await fs.mkdir(inputCenterRoot(),{recursive:true});await shell.openPath(inputCenterRoot());return {ok:true};}
1151: async function resolveInputAsset(fileValue=''){const raw=String(fileValue||'').trim();if(!raw)return '';const direct=path.isAbsolute(raw)?raw:path.join(stableUserDataRoot(),raw);try{await fs.access(direct);return direct;}catch{return '';}}
1152: async function readInputCenterAsset(fileValue=''){const filePath=await resolveInputAsset(fileValue);if(!filePath)return {ok:false,reason:'원본 파일을 찾지 못했습니다.'};const ext=path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();const mime={'.jpg':'image/jpeg','.jpeg':'image/jpeg','.png':'image/png','.webp':'image/webp','.gif':'image/gif'}[ext];if(!mime)return {ok:false,reason:'화면 미리보기는 JPG·PNG·WEBP 이미지를 지원합니다.',path:filePath};const data=await fs.readFile(filePath);return {ok:true,path:filePath,name:path.basename(filePath),dataUrl:`${'data:'}${mime};base64,${data.toString('base64')}`};}

1155: async function runProcess(command,args=[],options={}){
1156:   return new Promise((resolve)=>{
1157:     let settled=false;const finish=(v)=>{if(!settled){settled=true;resolve(v)}};
1158:     try{const child=spawn(command,args,{windowsHide:true,...options});let stdout='',stderr='';
1159:       child.stdout?.on('data',d=>stdout+=d.toString());child.stderr?.on('data',d=>stderr+=d.toString());
1160:       child.on('error',e=>finish({ok:false,code:-1,stdout,stderr:String(e?.message||e)}));
1161:       child.on('close',code=>finish({ok:code===0,code,stdout,stderr}));
1162:     }catch(e){finish({ok:false,code:-1,stdout:'',stderr:String(e?.message||e)})}
1163:   });
1164: }

1211: async function appendSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values) {
1212:   const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:append`);
1213:   url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
1214:   url.searchParams.set("insertDataOption", "INSERT_ROWS");
1215:   const response = await fetch(url, {
1216:     method: "POST",
1217:     headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
1218:     body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
1219:   });
1220:   if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공지 저장 실패 (${response.status})`);
1221:   return response.json();
1222: }

1224: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limit, worker) {
1225:   const queue = Array.from(items || []);
1226:   const results = new Array(queue.length);
1227:   let cursor = 0;
1228:   const runners = Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, Math.min(limit, queue.length || 1)) }, async () => {
1229:     while (true) {
1230:       const index = cursor++;
1231:       if (index >= queue.length) return;
1232:       results[index] = await worker(queue[index], index);
1233:     }
1234:   });
1235:   await Promise.all(runners);
1236:   return results;
1237: }

1252: function reportDateTimeText(value=""){
1253:   if(value instanceof Date&&!Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))return `${value.getFullYear()}-${String(value.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(value.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(value.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(value.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(value.getSeconds()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
1254:   const raw=String(value||"").trim();
1255:   const kr=raw.match(/^(\d{4})[.\/-]\s*(\d{1,2})[.\/-]\s*(\d{1,2})\.?\s*(오전|오후)?\s*(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/);
1256:   if(kr){let hour=Number(kr[5]);if(kr[4]==='오후'&&hour<12)hour+=12;if(kr[4]==='오전'&&hour===12)hour=0;return `${kr[1]}-${String(kr[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(kr[3]).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(hour).padStart(2,'0')}:${kr[6]}:${kr[7]||'00'}`;}
1257:   const m=raw.match(/^(\d{4})[./-](\d{1,2})[./-](\d{1,2})(?:\s+|T)(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/);
1258:   return m?`${m[1]}-${String(m[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(m[3]).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(m[4]).padStart(2,'0')}:${m[5]}:${m[6]||'00'}`:raw;
1259: }

3854: function accessAccountRole(account){
3855:   if(String(account?.admin||'').toUpperCase()==='Y')return '관리자';
3856:   const role=String(account?.role||'').trim();

3879:   document.body.appendChild(layer);
3880:   const close=()=>layer.remove(); layer.querySelectorAll('[data-access-close]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=close); layer.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===layer)close();};
3881:   layer.querySelector('[data-access-refresh]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{const result=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.();if(result?.ok){readonlyCache=result.data;layer.remove();openAccessControlPanel();}else toast(result?.reason||'사용자 계정표를 새로고침하지 못했습니다.');});
3882:   if(isAdmin){
3883:     layer.querySelectorAll('[data-access-user]').forEach(row=>{
3884:       const id=row.dataset.accessUser, account=accounts.find(a=>String(a.id)===String(id)); if(!account)return;
3885:       row.querySelector('[data-access-toggle]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{
3886:         if(account.active!==false && String(account.id)===String(p.id)) return toast('현재 로그인한 관리자 계정은 자기 자신을 중지할 수 없습니다.');
3887:         const active=account.active===false; const result=await window.schoolBoard?.updateUserAccount?.({id,active});
3888:         if(!result?.ok)return toast(result?.reason||'사용자 승인 상태를 저장하지 못했습니다.');
3889:         const refreshed=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.(); if(refreshed?.ok)readonlyCache=refreshed.data; layer.remove();openAccessControlPanel();toast(active?'사용 승인을 완료했습니다.':'사용을 중지했습니다.');
3890:       });
3891:       row.querySelector('[data-access-save]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{
3892:         const role=row.querySelector('[data-access-role]')?.value||'', grade=row.querySelector('[data-access-grade]')?.value||'1', classNo=row.querySelector('[data-access-class]')?.value||'';
3893:         const admin=role==='관리자'; const storedRole=admin?'관리자':role;
3894:         const result=await window.schoolBoard?.updateUserAccount?.({id,role:storedRole,admin,grade,classNo,active:account.active!==false});
3895:         if(!result?.ok)return toast(result?.reason||'권한을 저장하지 못했습니다.');
3896:         const refreshed=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.(); if(refreshed?.ok)readonlyCache=refreshed.data; layer.remove();openAccessControlPanel();toast('사용자 권한을 저장했습니다.');
3897:       });
3898:     });
3899:   }

3925: function accessControlInlineMarkup(){
3926:   const p=currentUserProfile(), isAdmin=currentRoleId()==="admin";
3927:   const accounts=[...(readonlyCache?.userAccounts||[])].sort((a,b)=>String(a.name||'').localeCompare(String(b.name||''),'ko'));
3928:   const activeCount=accounts.filter(a=>a.active!==false).length, blockedCount=accounts.length-activeCount;
3929:   const roleOptions=["관리자","학년부장","학년총무","담임교사","교과담당","교감","교장"];
3930:   const rows=accounts.length?accounts.map(a=>`<tr data-inline-access-user="${escapeHtml(a.id)}"><td><b>${escapeHtml(a.name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(maskEmail(a.email))}</small></td><td>${isAdmin?`<select data-inline-access-role>${roleOptions.map(r=>`<option ${accessAccountRole(a)===r?'selected':''}>${r}</option>`).join('')}</select>`:escapeHtml(a.admin==='Y'?'관리자':a.role||'교과담당')}</td><td>${isAdmin?`<select data-inline-access-grade>${[1,2,3].map(g=>`<option ${String(a.grade)==String(g)?'selected':''}>${g}</option>`).join('')}</select>`:escapeHtml(a.grade||'-')}</td><td>${isAdmin?`<select data-inline-access-class><option value="">-</option>${Array.from({length:14},(_,i)=>i+1).map(c=>`<option value="${c}" ${String(a.classNo)==String(c)?'selected':''}>${c}</option>`).join('')}</select>`:escapeHtml(a.classNo||'-')}</td><td><span class="state ${a.active!==false?'connected':'error'}">${a.active!==false?'승인':'차단'}</span></td><td>${isAdmin?`<button class="btn secondary" data-inline-access-toggle>${a.active!==false?'사용 중지':'사용 승인'}</button><button class="btn primary" data-inline-access-save>권한 저장</button>`:'-'}</td></tr>`).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="6">01_사용자계정에 등록된 사용자가 없습니다.</td></tr>';
3931:   return `<article class="setting-card access-control-inline"><div class="connection-title"><div><small>UEP RELEASE SECURITY</small><h3>사용자·권한 승인</h3><p>01_사용자계정의 사용여부·역할·담당학년·담임반을 이 화면에서 바로 관리합니다.</p></div><span class="state connected">계정표 승인</span></div><div class="security-control-grid release-security-grid"><article><small>등록 사용자</small><b>${accounts.length}명</b><p>01_사용자계정 기준</p></article><article><small>사용 승인</small><b>${activeCount}명</b><p>로그인 가능</p></article><article><small>사용 중지</small><b>${blockedCount}명</b><p>로그인 차단</p></article><article><small>현재 관리자</small><b>${escapeHtml(p.name||'-')}</b><p>${escapeHtml(currentRoleDisplay())}</p></article></div><div class="access-user-table-wrap"><table class="access-user-table"><thead><tr><th>사용자</th><th>역할</th><th>학년</th><th>담임반</th><th>상태</th><th>관리</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div><div class="connection-actions"><button class="btn secondary" data-inline-access-refresh>계정표 새로고침</button></div><p class="safe-note">현재 로그인한 관리자 자기 계정은 사용 중지할 수 없습니다.</p></article>`;
3932: }

5365: function normalizedSentenceTokens(text){
5366:   return new Set(String(text||"").replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s]/gu," ").split(/\s+/).filter(x=>x.length>=2));
5367: }
5368: function jaccardSimilarity(a,b){
5369:   const A=normalizedSentenceTokens(a),B=normalizedSentenceTokens(b);
5370:   if(!A.size||!B.size)return 0;
5371:   let both=0;A.forEach(x=>{if(B.has(x))both++;});
5372:   return both/(A.size+B.size-both);
5373: }

9245: function normalizeAdmissionMatchText(value){
9246:   return String(value||'').normalize('NFKC').toLowerCase().replace(/대학교/g,'대').replace(/[\s·ㆍ.,:;()\[\]{}'"`~!@#$%^&*+=_|\\/<>?–—-]+/g,'').trim();

9248: function admissionTrackKey(value){
9249:   const raw=String(value||'').normalize('NFKC').toLowerCase();
9250:   const text=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(raw);
9251:   if(!text)return '';
9252:   // 전형명은 학교별 표현이 달라도 같은 선발 방식이면 동일 전형군으로 연결합니다.
9253:   if(/학생부종합|학종|종합전형|학교생활우수|자기추천|참인재|do dream|do드림/.test(raw))return '학생부종합';
9254:   if(/논술/.test(raw))return '논술';
9255:   if(/특기|실기/.test(raw))return '실기특기';
9256:   if(/지역인재/.test(raw))return '지역인재';
9257:   if(/지역균형|학교장추천|학교추천|추천형|추천전형|고교추천|균형교과|고교연계|학생부교과|교과우수|교과전형|교과성적|교과100|교과 100/.test(raw))return '학생부교과';
9258:   return text;
9259: }
9260: function admissionUnitTokens(value){
9261:   const raw=String(value||'').normalize('NFKC').toLowerCase();
9262:   if(!raw.trim())return [];
9263:   const groups=[];
9264:   const tests=[
9265:     ['의예',/의예|의학|의과/],['치의예',/치의예|치의학|치과/],['약학',/약학|약대/],['한의예',/한의예|한의학/],
9266:     ['간호보건',/간호|보건|의료/],['공학',/공학|공과|공대|전자|전기|기계|컴퓨터|소프트웨어|인공지능|반도체|화공|신소재|건축|토목/],
9267:     ['자연',/자연|이공|과학|수학|물리|화학|생명|환경/],['인문사회',/인문|사회|상경|경영|경제|행정|정치|국문|영문|외국어|사학|철학|교육/],
9268:     ['예체능',/예체능|예술|체육|미술|음악/]
9269:   ];

9272: }
9273: function admissionPairCompatibility(cut,rule){
9274:   const cutTrack=admissionTrackKey(cut?.track||cut?.name), ruleTrack=admissionTrackKey(rule?.name||rule?.type);
9275:   const cutName=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(cut?.name), ruleName=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(rule?.name);
9276:   const cutUnit=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(cut?.unit), ruleUnit=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(rule?.unit);
9277:   const cutGroups=admissionUnitTokens(cut?.unit), ruleGroups=admissionUnitTokens(rule?.unit);
9278:   const trackExact=Boolean(cutName&&ruleName&&cutName===ruleName);
9279:   const trackGroup=Boolean(cutTrack&&ruleTrack&&cutTrack===ruleTrack);
9280:   const unitExact=Boolean(cutUnit&&ruleUnit&&(cutUnit===ruleUnit||cutUnit.includes(ruleUnit)||ruleUnit.includes(cutUnit)));
9281:   const unitGroup=Boolean(cutGroups.length&&ruleGroups.length&&cutGroups.some(x=>ruleGroups.includes(x)));
9282:   // 모집단위가 양쪽에 명시됐는데 계열까지 충돌하면 같은 근거로 묶지 않습니다.
9283:   if(cutUnit&&ruleUnit&&!unitExact&&cutGroups.length&&ruleGroups.length&&!unitGroup)return {ok:false,reason:'모집단위 불일치'};
9284:   // 전형명이 양쪽에 명시된 경우 정확일치 또는 표준 전형군 일치가 필요합니다.
9285:   if(cutName&&ruleName&&!trackExact&&!trackGroup)return {ok:false,reason:'전형군 불일치'};
9286:   if(trackExact)return {ok:true,level:4,reason:'전형명 정확일치'};
9287:   if(trackGroup&&unitExact)return {ok:true,level:4,reason:'전형군+모집단위 일치'};
9288:   if(trackGroup&&unitGroup)return {ok:true,level:3,reason:'전형군+모집계열 일치'};
9289:   if(trackGroup&&(!cutUnit||!ruleUnit))return {ok:true,level:2,reason:'전형군 일치·한쪽 모집단위 미기재'};
9290:   // 한 DB에 전형명이 비어 있어도 모집단위가 명확히 연결되면 허용합니다.
9291:   if((!cutName||!ruleName)&&(unitExact||unitGroup))return {ok:true,level:2,reason:unitExact?'모집단위 일치':'모집계열 일치'};
9292:   return {ok:false,reason:'동일 전형 근거 부족'};
9293: }
9294: function admissionPairCompatible(cut,rule){return admissionPairCompatibility(cut,rule).ok;}
9295: function strictAdmissionCutLimit(value){
9296:   if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return null;
9297:   const direct=Number(value);if(Number.isFinite(direct)&&direct>=1&&direct<=9)return direct;
9298:   const values=(String(value).replace(/,/g,'').match(/\d+(?:\.\d+)?/g)||[]).map(Number).filter(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=1&&v<=9);
9299:   if(!values.length)return null;
9300:   // 복수 숫자가 들어간 문자열은 더 엄격한 값(작은 등급)으로 판정하여 오탐을 막습니다.
9301:   return Math.min(...values);
9302: }

11098: function parseStudentLine(line) {
11099:   const trimmed = String(line || "").trim();
11100:   if (!trimmed) return { number: "", name: "" };
11101:   const parts = trimmed.includes("\t")
11102:     ? trimmed.split(/\t+/)
11103:     : trimmed.includes(",")
11104:       ? trimmed.split(/,+/)
11105:       : trimmed.split(/\s+/);
11106:   return { number: (parts.shift() || "").trim(), name: parts.join(" ").trim() };
11107: }
